## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试，或者帮你看看邮件和日历里有没有需要处理的事。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal partner on this Mac. For example, I can fix a flaky test in one of your repos and tell you when it's done.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 지금 바로 해볼 수 있는 일로는, 이 컴퓨터의 저장소에서 깨진 테스트를 찾아 고치는 작업을 맡길 수 있어요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは信頼性のある順序どおりの配送を保証し、UDPは保証せずに速さと軽さを優先します。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para poner al día tu propia rama privada con la base y mantener el historial lineal, pero nunca sobre commits que otros ya tienen.
Usa merge para integrar ramas compartidas o ya publicadas, porque conserva el historial tal cual ocurrió sin reescribir nada.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠传输：先建立连接，保证数据按序、不丢不重地送到；UDP 则不建连接、不做这些保证，尽快发出去，快但可能丢包或乱序。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own local, unpushed work to keep history clean before you share it. Merge once the branch is shared or public, since rewriting commits others already have causes problems.
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to a database in an append-only file before that change touches the main data files. If the system crashes midway, the database replays that log on restart to finish committed changes and discard incomplete ones, so no data is lost or left half-written.
```

  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户原话：「接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点」

要做的：
- 找到 review 里关于锁粒度的意见，把相应的锁改细（缩小临界区，或把一把大锁拆成更细的锁），保持 compaction 的正确性不变。
- 跑相关测试，把结果（通过/失败的数量和关键输出）报回来。
- 改动提交在这个任务自己的分支上，并在报告里写明改了哪些地方、为什么这样拆。

不做的：
- 不合并到主分支，不推送，不把 draft PR 改成 ready。这些等用户点头。
- 不改 compaction 之外的逻辑。

地方：沿用这个任务原来的仓库和分支。
```

  [partner message] 做好了，周五京沪高铁的看板已经填上：600 元以下现在有 2 班，最低 553 元，之后每天早上跟着更新。
  [an app card under the message: made "周五京沪高铁票价", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1653 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">周五北京到上海 · 600 元以下</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="cnt">还没看</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">每天早上看一次票价，结果更新在这里</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">班次</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
var LIMIT = 600;
function fmt(n){ return '¥' + n; }
function render(){
  var trains = ducter.get('trains') || [];
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var cnt = document.getElementById('cnt');
  var sub = document.getElementById('sub');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  if (!trains.length) {
    cnt.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">还没记</span>';
    return;
  }
  var under = trains.filter(function(t){ return t.price < LIMIT; });
  var min = under.length ? Math.min.apply(null, under.map(function(t){ return t.price; })) : null;
  cnt.textContent = under.length + ' 班';
  sub.textContent = min !== null ? '最低 ' + fmt(min) + ' · 每天早上更新' : '暂时没有 600 元以下的班次';
  trains.forEach(function(t){
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.textContent = t.no;
    var s = document.createElement('div');
    s.className = 'k-row-sub';
    s.textContent = t.seats || '';
    main.appendChild(s);
    var v = document.createElement('div');
    v.className = 'k-row-value';
    v.style.color = t.price < LIMIT ? 'var(--d-good)' : 'var(--d-muted)';
    v.textContent = fmt(t.price);
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(v);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"trains":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"seats":"二等座余 12 张"},{"no":"G3","price":553,"seats":"二等座余 4 张"},{"no":"G7","price":626,"seats":"二等座"}]}

  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2088 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest">—</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latestSub">writes/s · p99 latency</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">Each night</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</div>
<style>
  body{background:var(--d-bg);color:var(--d-ink);font-family:inherit}
  .k-row{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:10px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--d-line)}
  .k-row:last-child{border-bottom:none}
  .k-row-main{font-weight:600}
  .k-row-sub{color:var(--d-muted);font-size:13px}
  .k-row-value{text-align:right;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
</style>
<script>
  function render(nights){
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    list.innerHTML = '';
    if(!nights || !nights.length){
      list.innerHTML = '<div class="k-empty">Nothing yet: the first night\'s result will show here.</div>';
      document.getElementById('latest').innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">Not yet</span>';
      document.getElementById('latestSub').textContent = 'writes/s · p99 latency';
      return;
    }
    var last = nights[nights.length-1];
    document.getElementById('latest').textContent = last.writes + ' writes/s';
    document.getElementById('latestSub').textContent = 'p99 ' + last.p99 + ' · compaction stalls ' + last.stalls;
    nights.slice().reverse().forEach(function(n){
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      row.innerHTML = '<div><div class="k-row-main"></div><div class="k-row-sub"></div></div><div class="k-row-value"></div>';
      row.querySelector('.k-row-main').textContent = n.night;
      row.querySelector('.k-row-sub').textContent = 'p99 ' + n.p99 + ' · stalls ' + n.stalls;
      row.querySelector('.k-row-value').textContent = n.writes + '/s';
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
  }
  render(ducter.get('nights'));
  ducter.onChange(function(){ render(ducter.get('nights')); });
  ducter.summary('Night 3: 182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms');
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"night":"Night 2","writes":"181k","p99":"4.0 ms","stalls":0},{"night":"Night 3","writes":"182k","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":0}]}

      return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c];

    if (!day || !what || isNaN(amount)) return;
    items.push({
      id: String(Date.now()) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6),
      day: day,
      what: what,
      amount: amount
    });
    save().then(function () {
      document.getElementById('what').value = '';
      document.getElementById('amt').value = '';
      render();

  });

  document.getElementById('days').addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var row = e.target.closest('[data-id]');
    if (!row) return;
    var id = row.getAttribute('data-id');
    var item = items.find(function (it) { return it.id === id; });
    if (!item) return;
    Promise.resolve(ducter.ask('删掉「' + item.what + ' ' + fmt(item.amount) + '」这笔？')).then(function (ans) {
      if (ans && String(ans).indexOf('删') !== -1) {
        items = items.filter(function (it) { return it.id !== id; });
        save().then(render);
      }
    });
  });

  if (typeof ducter.onChange === 'function') {
    ducter.onChange(load);
  }
  load();
})();

<script>
function fmt(n){return "¥"+n.toLocaleString("zh-CN");}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+(Number(x.yuan)||0),0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent=xs.length?fmt(total):"还没记";
  if(!xs.length) document.getElementById("total").classList.add("k-none");
  else document.getElementById("total").classList.remove("k-none");
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"北京出差的花销会列在这里";
  ducter.summary(xs.length? xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(total) : "还没记");
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML="";
  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");
    row.className="k-row";
    row.innerHTML='<div class="k-row-main"></div><div class="k-row-sub"></div><div class="k-row-value"></div>';
    row.querySelector(".k-row-main").textContent=x.what;
    row.querySelector(".k-row-sub").textContent=x.day;
    row.querySelector(".k-row-value").textContent=fmt(Number(x.yuan));
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
}
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

      <button class="k-btn" id="cancel" hidden>取消</button>
    </div>
  </div>
</details>
</main>

<style>
  body { background: var(--d-bg); color: var(--d-ink); }
  .k-row { width: 100%; background: none; border: 0; color: inherit; font: inherit; cursor: pointer; display: fle … ime').classList.toggle('bad', !okTime);
    $('#ml').classList.toggle('bad', !okMl);
    if (!okTime || !okMl) return;
    if (editId !== null) {
      const f = feeds.find(x => x.id === editId);
      if (f) { f.time = normTime(time); f.ml = ml; }
    } else {
      feeds.push({ id: Date.now(), date: todayStr, time: normTime(time), ml: ml });
    }
    persist();
    resetForm();
  };

  $('#del').onclick = () => {
    feeds = feeds.filter(x => x.id !== editId);
    persist();
    resetForm();
  };
  $('#cancel').onclick = resetForm;

  $('#list').onclick = e => {
    const btn = e.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (!btn) return;
    const f = feeds.find(x => x.id === +btn.dataset.id);
    if (!f) return;
    editId = f.id;
    $('#time').value = f.time;
    $('#ml').value = f.ml;
    $('#save').textContent = '保存修改';
    $('#del').hidden = false;
    $('#cancel').hidden = false;
    $('#add').open = true;
    updateChips();
    $('#add').scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  };

  function load() {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('feeds')).then(v => {
      feeds = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
      render();
    });
  }

  ducter.onChange(load);
  resetForm();
  load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"id":1,"date":"2026-11-24","time":"06:10","ml":120},{"id":2,"date":"2026-11-24","time":"09:05","ml":90}]}

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Here's a page with your four open PRs. Tap a row when it lands and the count updates.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2022 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still open</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="left">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">tap a row when it lands</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">My PRs</h2>
  <div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
</main>
<script>
const DEFAULT = ducter.get("default") || [];
function items(){ const v = ducter.get('prs'); return Array.isArray(v) ? v : DEFAULT; }
function save(list){ ducter.set('prs', list); }
function render(){
  const list = items();
  const left = list.filter(p => !p.done).length;
  document.getElementById('left').textContent = String(left);
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent = left ? 'tap a row when it lands' : 'all landed';
  const box = document.getElementById('list');
  box.textContent = '';
  list.forEach(p => {
    const row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    row.style.cursor = 'pointer';
    const mark = document.createElement('span');
    mark.textContent = p.done ? '☑' : '☐';
    mark.style.fontSize = '20px';
    mark.style.marginRight = '10px';
    const main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.style.flex = '1';
    const t = document.createElement('div');
    t.textContent = p.what + (p.done ? ' (landed)' : '');
    if (p.done) t.style.textDecoration = 'line-through';
    const sub = document.createElement('div');
    sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
    sub.textContent = p.repo + ' · ' + p.state;
    main.appendChild(t);
    main.appendChild(sub);
    row.style.display = 'flex';
    row.style.alignItems = 'center';
    row.appendChild(mark);
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const next = items().map(x => x.id === p.id ? Object.assign({}, x, {done: !x.done}) : x);
      save(next);
      render();
    });
    box.appendChild(row);
  });
  ducter.summary(left + ' open, ' + (list.length - left) + ' landed');
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"default":[{"id":"ducter412","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#412","what":"Compaction cut","state":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday","done":false},{"id":"mem10287","repo":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","what":"Retry on 502","state":"CI failing","done":false},{"id":"hawdb1592","repo":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","what":"Flaky compaction test","state":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday","done":false},{"id":"ducter418","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#418","what":"Apps bar","state":"Draft","done":false}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

请在 ~/dev/foo 的 TTL 测试里再补一条测试，覆盖缓存项恰好到期的那一刻（假时钟推进到刚好等于过期时间）：
- 到期时刻本身：应视为已过期（或按现有实现的语义，保持一致并在测试里写明）。
- 到期前一瞬间（比如少 1ms 或最小单位）：仍然有效。
- 沿用现在的假时钟，不要用真实 sleep。
- 验证：跑一遍 TTL 测试，并连续跑多次确认稳定，把结果报告回来。
- 范围外：不要合并、不要推送、不要开 PR；改动留在现在的分支上提交。
